<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="52320-decolonial-cyberwarfare----ukrainian-digital-sovereignty-and-corporate-power">
                    De&shy;co&shy;lo&shy;ni&shy;al cy&shy;ber&shy;war&shy;fare -- Ukrain&shy;i&shy;an di&shy;git&shy;al sov&shy;er&shy;eignty and cor&shy;por&shy;ate power
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:&nbsp;</strong><br>Ukraine has been historically targeted by some of the most destructive state-sponsored cyberattacks on record, and since the Russian full-scale invasion on 24 February 2022 has received considerable technological support from powerful foreign corporations. However, most research on the topic focuses on these (mostly American) actors, rather than on Ukraine's own efforts to assert its own sovereignty through the digital sphere, in a context of Russian imperialistic war (both kinetic and cyber) and critical dependency on foreign technology businesses. &nbsp;</p>
<p>This project investigates how Ukrainian policymakers, technology practitioners, and private companies construct and conceptualise Ukraine's digital sovereignty -- a term not officially used yet informally widely discussed -- as supporting the country's territorial integrity, institutional values, and decolonisation efforts. The project is conducted through a year-long fieldwork period in Ukraine and contributes to new understandings of digital sovereignty, a recent cornerstone of EU policy that will likely be influenced by Ukrainian experiences, as well to broader knowledge about cyber operations in wartime and the Russo-Ukrainian war.</p>
<p><strong>Project team:&nbsp;</strong><br>MSC and TechSoc cluster</p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners:&nbsp;</strong><br>Institute of International Relations National Taras Shevchenko University of Kyiv&nbsp;</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong>&nbsp;<br>DFF (2026-2028)</p>
<p><strong>For more information</strong>, please contact<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/vic-castro" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="dbaa065e-319e-4676-87c4-d53457dccf06" data-entity-substitution="canonical"> Vic Castro</a> <a href="mailto:vc.msc@cbs.dk">vc.msc@cbs.dk</a></p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="52318-the-crisis-of-migration-discourse">
                    The Crisis of Mi&shy;gra&shy;tion Dis&shy;course
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>A participatory approach for a new lexicon of migration.<br>The Crisis of Migration Discourse (CMD) is a&nbsp;<a href="https://chanse.org/">HERA/CHANSE (HORIZON 2020) funded European project.&nbsp;</a>CMD engages with the impact of discourses and policies that construct migration through the lens of crises on people using unauthorised routes to enter the EU and the UK and are as a result illegalised. To do so, CMD posits illegalised migrants as agents of change working with them to impact the current narratives on migration and related practices and to enhance intercultural exchanges.</p>
<p>The project is carried out across five European countries (Italy, France, Denmark, Spain and the United Kingdom), working with migrants, NGOs, local communities, journalists and policy stakeholders.<br><br><strong>Project team:</strong><br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/anne-vestergaard" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="52084826-bdf9-43bc-b46d-ebf47599502b" data-entity-substitution="canonical">PI Anne Vestergaard</a>, MSC<br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/ludmila-bogdan" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="5c06786f-d300-4cb9-b951-4936297a8286" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Postdoc Ludmila Bogdan,</a> MSC<br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/elena-horton" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="d2ddf5dd-7621-498e-a33b-77580d7be2eb" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Practice Researcher Elena Horton</a>, MSC<br>International Project Lead: Roza Tsagarourianou, University of Westminster</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>HERA/CHANCE (HORIZON 2020), 2025-2028&nbsp;<br><br><strong>For more information</strong>, please contact Anne Vestergaard. <a href="mailto:Av.msc@cbs.dk">Av.msc@cbs.dk</a></p>
<p><a href="https://thecrisisofmigrationdiscourse.co.uk">https://thecrisisofmigrationdiscourse.co.uk</a></p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="52322-rethinking-finance-can-democracy-enhance-sustainable-investments">
                    Re&shy;think&shy;ing Fin&shy;ance: Can Demo&shy;cracy En&shy;hance Sus&shy;tain&shy;able In&shy;vest&shy;ments?
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p>The green transition of contemporary capitalism is in dire straits: while a swift transition from fossil fuels to green energy is urgently needed, shaky geopolitics instead spurs uncertainty and defense investments. To navigate how sustainable investments can be encouraged and legitimized, the literature lacks studies on divestment (fossil fuel dis-investment) decision determinants, democratic voice in institutional investment choices, and influential civil society divestment campaigns. At the center of today’s financialized economy are pension funds, as they are the largest asset owner group and active owners, historically. Containing significant internal variances, the comparatively advanced climate-related investment choices and member democratic structures of Danish pension funds, along with the relatively impactful Danish divestment campaign, constitute a unique laboratory for filling these gaps. Therefore, this project explores: To what extent have the funds’ climate-related investment decisions been shaped by their type and degree of democracy, the divestment campaign’s strategies and pension member organization, fund-specific member groups and individuals, and prevalent financial frameworks? This is examined through a mixture of interviews with trustees, administrators, regulators, and NGOs, discourse analysis of reports and media statements, and comparisons of decisions, strategies, and actual investments at different historical stages.</p>
<p><strong>Project team:</strong></p>
<p><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/johannes-lundberg" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="7eb8da4d-0fd4-44a4-9a93-8b3d7990dd59" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Johannes Lundberg (PI)</a></p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners:</strong></p>
<p>Linda Soneryd (SCORE, Stockholm University)</p>
<p>Fossil Free Futures: Divestment Across the Nordic Countries (Research project) (project description:&nbsp;<a href="https://eur02.safelinks.protection.outlook.com/?url=https%3A%2F%2Ffuturenordics.org%2Fprojects%2Ffossil-free-futures-divestment-across-nordic-countries&amp;data=05%7C02%7Ccjc.msc%40cbs.dk%7C3c33d1bc5fa44e8a204108deebd228c5%7C875c414e5d004cdbb77adeae5d6ab201%7C0%7C0%7C639207485082156718%7CUnknown%7CTWFpbGZsb3d8eyJFbXB0eU1hcGkiOnRydWUsIlYiOiIwLjAuMDAwMCIsIlAiOiJXaW4zMiIsIkFOIjoiTWFpbCIsIldUIjoyfQ%3D%3D%7C0%7C%7C%7C&amp;sdata=RDKPNU6oHfa5MRF%2Fnw2WFgUmAOU8%2F2Y1eADU998UTR0%3D&amp;reserved=0">https://futurenordics.org/projects/fossil-free-futures-divestment-across-nordic-countries</a>)</p>
<p><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/kristian-bondo-hansen" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="e5f7cbcd-bf0b-40e0-a562-e91ef18ea138" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Kristian Bondo Hansen (MSC, CBS)</a></p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong></p>
<p>Carlsbergfondet,&nbsp;Internationalisation Fellowship</p>
<p>Project start and end: 2025-2028</p>
<p><strong>For more information,&nbsp;</strong>please contact&nbsp;<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/johannes-lundberg" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="7eb8da4d-0fd4-44a4-9a93-8b3d7990dd59" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Johannes Lundberg:</a>&nbsp;<a href="mailto:jol.msc@cbs.dk">jol.msc@cbs.dk</a></p>
<p>Link to Carlsberg’s project description:&nbsp;<a href="https://www.carlsbergfondet.dk/det-har-vi-stoettet/cf25-0471/">https://www.carlsbergfondet.dk/det-har-vi-stoettet/cf25-0471/</a>&nbsp;</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="1946-cir4fun">
                    Cir4Fun 
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p lang="DA-DK"><strong>The project:&nbsp;</strong><br>The European furniture industry is transitioning towards sustainability and circular practices, driven by the need for regulatory alignment and standardisation. The EU-funded Cir4Fun project will enhance furniture sustainability by introducing digital product passports (DPP) and promoting eco-labelling. It will create a circular economy roadmap, develop eco-design guidelines and establish innovative business models. The project will also devise new methodologies for assessing maintenance, reparability, refurbishment, remanufacturing and recyclability. These insights will be integrated into an interoperable furniture assessment system (FAS), featuring a sustainable index system, developed with the support of life cycle assessment, life cycle costing and social life cycle assessment under a Furniture Data Space. Cir4Fun will actively engage stakeholders to foster sustainable behaviours, advancing the industry’s shift to circularity.&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>Project team:&nbsp;</strong><br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/isabel-froes" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="db3bf87c-1346-47ff-8f74-ae93a7309253" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Isabel Froes</a>&nbsp;and&nbsp;<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/sebastian-olsson" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="32c591c6-7b97-4275-8eac-1a2716259016" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Sebastian Olsson&nbsp;</a></p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>Collaborative partners:&nbsp;</strong><br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;INSTITUTO TECNOLOGICO METALMECANICO, MUEBLE, MADERA, EMBALAJE Y AFINES-AIDIMME&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;ETHNIKO KENTRO EREVNAS KAI TECHNOLOGIKIS ANAPTYXIS&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;UNINOVA-INSTITUTO DE DESENVOLVIMENTO DE NOVAS TECNOLOGIAS-ASSOCIACAO&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;POLITECNICO DI MILANO&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;FRAUNHOFER GESELLSCHAFT ZUR FORDERUNG DER ANGEWANDTEN FORSCHUNG EV&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;COMMISSARIAT A L ENERGIE ATOMIQUE ET AUX ENERGIES ALTERNATIVES&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;XGILITY LIMITED&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;F6S EU TECH INNOVATION NETWORK DESIGNATED ACTIVITY COMPANY&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;F6S NETWORK LIMITED&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;KENTRO IKANOTITON GIA TIN VIOMICHANIA 4.0 APO TO SCHEDIASMO STIN YLOPOIISI IDIOTIKI KEFALAIOUCHIKI ETAIREIA&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;SONAE ARAUCO PORTUGAL SA&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;MICUNA FAMILY BRANDS S.L.U.&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;Kave Home SL&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;OPENDOT SRL&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;CENTRO PARA A VALORIZACAO DE RESIDUOS ASSOCIACAO&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;Pozzi Arturo S.p.A. TESSITURA JACQUARD&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;ELLINIKI DIAHEIRISI APPRIMATON ANONYMI ETAIRIA - ELDIA&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;MUNICIPIO DE GUIMARAES&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;FEDERACION EMPRESARIAL DE LA MADERAY MUEBLE DE LA COMUNIDAD VALENCIANA - (FEVAMA)&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;ENOSI KATANALOTON POIOTITA TIS ZOIS&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;SARA SA&nbsp;<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;SCUOLA UNIVERSITARIA PROFESSIONALE DELLA SVIZZERA ITALIANA&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>The grant:</strong><br>EU, Grant agreement ID: 101182081. Start date: 1. January. 2025. End date: 31. December 2027.&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>For more information</strong>, please contact<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/sebastian-olsson" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="32c591c6-7b97-4275-8eac-1a2716259016" data-entity-substitution="canonical">&nbsp;Sebastian Olsson</a> at&nbsp;<a href="mailto:seo.msc@cbs.dk">seo.msc@cbs.dk</a>&nbsp;</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>


        </div>
      
        <div class="inner-sub-components">
          


  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="1948-vision4food">
                    Vis&shy;ion4Food
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>The&nbsp;VISION4FOOD project is envisioning an integrated quadruple helix and RRI framework for food system transformation and regional innovation ecosystem enhancement. It brings together 13 partners from 8 countries, united by a shared mission: to transform and strengthen the sustainability and resilience of EU food systems.&nbsp;</p>
<p>The project is collaborating across 5 diverse regions - Finland, Spain, Italy, Greece, and Poland - to develop innovative governance models that empower local food systems. By uniting Quadruple Helix stakeholders, we're enabling the creation of Food Innovation Platforms (FIPs) designed to scale innovations and drive transformative change in food systems.&nbsp;</p>
<p>Vision4Food approach focuses on co-creating research and innovation strategies, promoting open science, and ensuring Responsible Research Innovation (RRI). Each region will receive tailored support for knowledge exchange, networking, and priority setting, accelerating the transition to more inclusive, innovative, and sustainable food systems.</p>
<p><strong>MSC project team:</strong><br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/isabel-froes" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="db3bf87c-1346-47ff-8f74-ae93a7309253" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Isabel Froes,</a> <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/albina-dioba" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="adef96e8-531e-42a4-93c6-f13e1d146640" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Albina Dioba</a></p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>The&nbsp;European Research Executive Agency (REA) under grant agreement No° 101183145. Funded by the European Union, 01.01.2025 - 31.12.2027.</p>
<p><strong>For more information,</strong> please visit the project's <a href="http://vision4foodproject.eu/">website</a>.</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="1950-the-seizmic-doctoral-network">
                    The seizm&shy;ic Doc&shy;tor&shy;al Net&shy;work
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>The seizmic Doctoral Network is a project funded by the Marie Skłodowska-Curie Actions (MSCA) with the aim of advancing the understanding and practice of scaling social entrepreneurship.<br>To address this challenge, seizmic integrates research and practitioner expertise in impact investing, business models, education, and Digital Ecosystems and AI, bringing together universities and industry partners to train a new generation of scholars.</p>
<p><strong>MSC project team:</strong><br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/kai-hockerts" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="b0d21e0c-2059-4eae-96ea-2d27a47e57bd" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Kai Hockerts</a> (Principal Investigator), <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/ricky-celenta" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="fc76745b-f859-41b9-b5eb-2f5a35ff4799" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Ricky Celenta</a> (Project Manager), <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/esben-rahbek-gjerdrum-pedersen" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="e611a2cd-e021-4511-8248-97ba94546a59" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Esben Rahbek Gjerdrum Pedersen</a>, <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/liana-razmerita" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="3f4e7c55-1206-47cd-918f-0b45d93d68c6" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Liana Razmerita</a>, <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/kristian-roed-nielsen" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="7b60c142-d2ec-485c-8d08-e36dbe9840e3" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Kristian Roed Nielsen</a> and <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/anne-karen-huske" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="d3a0f6a1-37af-4bae-b15c-3ea3ed761e03" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Anne-Karen Hüske</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners:</strong><br>The beneficiaries of the seizmic Doctoral Network include Aalborg University, ESCP Business School Berlin, Karazin University, Université Paris-Est Créteil (UPEC), University Federico II Naples, University of Iceland, University of Innsbruck, and University Rovira i Virgili in Catalonia.<br>The network also includes several associate partners, such as Aurora, Babele, Den Sociale Kapitalfond, Eranos, the Executive Committee of Boiarka City Council, the Iceland Ministry of Education, Impact Hub Berlin, KLAK, Ship2B, and the Società per l’Innovazione, la Cooperazione e l’Internazionalizzazione (SPICI srl).</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>This project has received funding from the European Union’s Horizon 2020 research and innovation programme under the Marie Skłodowska-Curie grant agreement No 101169098.</p>
<p><strong>Project duration:&nbsp;</strong><br>October 2024 - October 2028&nbsp;</p>
<p><strong>For more information,</strong> please contact <a href="mailto:rc.msc@cbs.dk">rc.msc@cbs.dk</a></p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="24455-sensopad">
                    SENSOPAD
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>The SENSOPAD project aims to transform women’s health by tackling the challenges of early Endometriosis (ED) detection, a condition often underdiagnosed. By introducing innovative, user-friendly devices that monitor ED indicators during the menstrual cycle, SENSOPAD offers a seamless combination of at-home convenience and clinical accuracy. Through a mobile app and AI-enhanced cloud platform, the project aims to reduce the typical ED diagnosis time from years to days, enabling earlier treatment and improving women’s health outcomes. This initiative promotes proactive, informed, and accessible healthcare for women worldwide.<br>As a task leader, CBS will guide the qualitative research process, conducting interviews with clinicians and menstruators and gathering user stories. CBS will also lead a large-scale European study to identify drivers and barriers to product adoption, ensuring the product meets the needs and preferences of diverse user groups.</p>
<p><strong>MSC project team:</strong><br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/angelos-stamos" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="4d5bd404-025a-4c87-9f07-23d073aa292b" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Angelos Stamos</a>, <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/isabel-froes" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="db3bf87c-1346-47ff-8f74-ae93a7309253" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Isabel Froes</a>, and Amalia Giannakopoulou.</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>The project is funded by the EU Horizon Europe Research and Innovation Program with a total budget of €6.8 million. It started in September 2024 and will run until August 2028.</p>
<p>Grant agreement ID: 101130516</p>
<p><strong>For more information</strong>, please&nbsp;contact<a href=" Angelos Stamos"> Angelos Stamos</a> (<a href="mailto:asta.msc@cbs.dk">asta.msc@cbs.dk</a>)</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="26239-frontiers-frontiers-of-natural-resource-and-sustainability-governance-for-a-just-energy-transition">
                    FRON&shy;TI&shy;ERS: Fron&shy;ti&shy;ers of nat&shy;ur&shy;al re&shy;source and sustain&shy;ability gov&shy;ernance for a just en&shy;ergy trans&shy;ition
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>Advancing knowledge for a just transition involving and respecting those at risk or in practice adversely affected by transition minerals mining, the FRONTIERS project explores practices in the mining industry and how institutional investors can shape that industry and its impact. Looking at land-based and potential deep-sea mining (DSM), we focus on effects and synergy of risk-based due diligence demands and Rights of Nature claims.<br>With the aim of developing knowledge that can serve towards a just and fair transition, FRONTIERS aims at developing novel insights into good practices that may serve as inspiration or guidance for various types of actors and stakeholders (human individuals and communities, organisations, and ‘more-than-human’ stakeholders, such as the environment and nature). As part of this, we explore how due diligence and forms of engagement in diverse organizational, normative and regulatory settings can contribute to giving a voice to affected stakeholders, whether human or ‘more-than-human’.<br>For more details, please see <a href="https://frontiers.cbs.dk/"><strong>https://frontiers.cbs.dk/</strong></a></p>
<p><strong>Project team:</strong><br>PI: Professor <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/karin-buhmann" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="c250cde0-289f-410f-be12-49d9ea517c5d" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Karin Buhmann</a><br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/jakob-lutz" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="db46ce92-f942-4f53-b06b-00abf26cb787" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Jakob Lutz</a>, PhD candidate<br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/helga-askgaard-estrup" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="8f20e85d-9e00-4cc2-894d-2fe4b61bc06f" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Helga Askgaard Estrup</a>, PhD candidate<br><a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/raphael-deberdt" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="6ac67493-19bb-4bae-ad79-908da875a464" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Raphael Deberdt</a>, Postdoc<br>Postdoc: currently being recruited.</p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners:</strong><br>VISITING SCHOLARS<br>FRONTIERS will involve collaboration with several visiting scholars. These include Dr. Samantha Goethals, SKEMA Business School, France, and expert in risk-based due diligence; Professor Kristina Siig, University of Southern Denmark, an expert in maritime and private law; and Dr Kevin Lo, an expert in Environmental Politics, Environmental Governance, Political Geography, Urban Geography, China;<br>&nbsp;<br>ACADEMIC ADVISORY BOARD<br>FRONTIERS will benefit from the expertise and suggestions from an interdisciplinary Academic Advisory Board:<br>Prof. <a href="https://www.edhec.edu/en/research-and-faculty/faculty/professors-and-researchers/bjorn-fasterling"><strong>Björn Fasterling</strong></a>, Professor in Law and Business Ethics, EDHEC Business School, France. Expertise in corporate sustainability due diligence in soft and hard law, business governance, and business ethics.<br>Dr. <a href="https://novalaw.unl.pt/en/claire-bright/"><strong>Claire Bright</strong></a>, Associate Professor, founder and director of the Centre for Business, Human Rights and the Environment (BHRE) at NOVA University, Lisbon. Expertise in EU law on corporate sustainability due diligence, just transitions, legal method including doctrinal studies.&nbsp;<br>Prof. <a href="https://www.jus.uio.no/nifs/personer/vit/olame/"><strong>Ola Mestad</strong></a> is Professor in public law and Director of the Scandinavian Institute of Maritime Law, University of Oslo. Expertise in international law including Law of the Sea; energy and natural resource extraction law; sustainable investing; Indigenous and community rights.&nbsp;<br>Prof. <a href="https://www.mun.ca/sociology/people/core-faculty/mark-cj-stoddart/"><strong>Mark Stoddart</strong></a>, Professor in Sociology, Memorial University, Canada. Expertise in<br>eco-politics, social movements, and environmental governance in relation to climate change and energy, including methodology for field work and interdisciplinary research.<br>&nbsp;<br>OTHER PARTNERS<br>The FRONTIERS research team will collaborate closely with several national and international partners. These include:<br>AkademikerPension, a Danish pension fund which is globally leading in sustainable finance.<br>Professor Claudia Marconi, is a political scientist and international relations scholar and lead of the Jean Monnet Centre of Excellence on Business and Human Rights at FECAP Business School, Sao Paolo, Brazil. Established in 2023 with funding from the European Commission, the centre is the first-ever Centre of Excellence on Business and Human Rights.<br>Dr. Alberto Fonseca, Associate Professor at the Federal University of Ouro Preto, Brazil. Fonseca is an expert in environmental and social impact assessment, with a back-ground in engineering and planning and a social science methods leaning, and a former president and scientific director of the Brazilian Association for Impact Assessment.</p>
<p><strong>Project start and end year, and funding sources:</strong><br>The project runs April 2024-March 2029. The project budget of DKK 9,996,438 is funded by the Semper Ardens Accomplish modality (Carlsberg Foundation). The Office of the CBS Dean of Research contributes another 10 % of that amount towards internal administrative costs etc.&nbsp;</p>
<p><strong>For more information</strong>, please contact the PI, Professor <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/karin-buhmann" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="c250cde0-289f-410f-be12-49d9ea517c5d" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Karin Buhmann</a> (<a href="mailto:kbu.msc@cbs.dk"><strong>kbu.msc@cbs.dk</strong></a>) and visit <a href="https://frontiers.cbs.dk/en/%C2%A0"><strong>https://frontiers.cbs.dk/en/&nbsp;</strong></a></p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="39660-euridice-european-inclusive-education-for-digital-society-social-innovation-and-global-citizenship-">
                    EUR&shy;IDICE: EURopean In&shy;clus&shy;ive Edu&shy;ca&shy;tion for Di&shy;git&shy;al So&shy;ci&shy;ety, So&shy;cial In&shy;nov&shy;a&shy;tion and Glob&shy;al Cit&shy;izEn&shy;ship 
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br><a href="https://euridice.eu/"><strong>EURIDICE </strong></a>is a European initiative co-funded by the EU’s Digital Europe Program, dedicated to fostering inclusive education in advanced digital skills.&nbsp;</p>
<p>Its overall objective is to educate and build in an interdisciplinary way the coming generation of social innovators with advanced digital technologies, with an emphasis on the highly societally influential sectors of <strong>Education, Communication and Culture</strong>.&nbsp;</p>
<p>The project supports multidisciplinary learning, bridging the gap between technology and humanities, and offers a European Joint Master’s degree, teacher training, and professional lifelong learning programs. Through its Collaboratorium for Blended International Hybrid Learning, EURIDICE integrates cutting-edge digital infrastructure for an innovative and collaborative educational experience.<br>With a strong focus on digital inclusion, ethical innovation, and human-centered technology, EURIDICE builds sustainable partnerships with academia, institutes, and SMEs, contributing to Europe’s digital sovereignty and the EU’s vision of a just and democratic digital society.</p>
<p><strong>Project team:</strong><br><a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/lramsc"><strong>Liana Razmerita</strong></a>, <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/dhamsc"><strong>Daniel Hardt</strong></a>,<a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/khomsc"><strong> Kai Hockerts</strong></a>, <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-business-humanities-and-law/staff/rjbhl"><strong>Rasmus Johnsen</strong></a></p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners:</strong><br>11 research-intensive universities across Europe, 2 independent digital research centers of excellence, 6 SMEs, and 4 associated HEI partners esp. from the Global South.&nbsp;</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>EU’s Digital Europe Program</p>
<p><strong>For more information</strong>, please contact&nbsp;<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/liana-razmerita" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="3f4e7c55-1206-47cd-918f-0b45d93d68c6" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Liana Razmerita</a> at <a href="mailto:lra.msc@cbs.dk"><strong>lra.msc@cbs.dk</strong></a>&nbsp;</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="26241-sustainable-engaging-and-creative-tourism-as-a-driver-for-a-better-future-in-rural-and-remote-areas-secretour-">
                    Sus&shy;tain&shy;able En&shy;ga&shy;ging and Cre&shy;at&shy;ive Tour&shy;ism as a driver for a bet&shy;ter fu&shy;ture in rur&shy;al and re&shy;mote areas (SECre&shy;Tour) 
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>The&nbsp;Horizon Europe project SECreTour focuses on cultural tourism in the European peripheries. In the project, Tourism is conceived as a tool to complement and diversify the income of the territories, a way of giving visibility and recognition to rural areas and their inhabitants, and a means to promote services that are beneficial both for the local communities and for the visitors. In this light, SECreTour will primarily focus on the local communities’ needs, perceptions, and expectations.<br>Tourism is more than travelling and consuming, and it has a great potential for sustainable development when it focuses on culture, nature, knowledge, and experiences. By developing a fair, creative and sustainable tourism approach together with the heritage communities, the SECreTour consortium aims to assess different local contexts, needs, and types of cultural heritage.<br>Specific goals are taken into account by the project while testing and experimenting with new forms of tourism development, namely: to avoid touristification, to promote alternative business models, to enable governance and citizen engagement not only for touristic-economic planning, but also for community building and cultural heritage management and protection.</p>
<p>Through eight pilot cases, the project aims to demonstrate how cultural heritage can be used as a real driver for sustainable and fair development, promoting at the same time its conservation. Pilots have been carefully chosen to represent a full range of European territories, communities, and heritage, including not only rural and agrarian landscapes, but also memory places of local identities, minorities, and conflictive dark heritage. The pilots represent the focus for every part of the research as they enable the testing of general ideas and observations in local detail and in specific governance contexts, and facilitate effective communication, cooperation and problem-solving through an interdisciplinary and trans-sectoral approach.</p>
<p><strong>MSC project team:</strong><br>The&nbsp;MSC SECreTour team consists of Associate Professor <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/cjhmsc"><strong>Carsten Humlebæk </strong></a>(local PI), Professor <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/erpgmsc"><strong>Esben Rahbek Gjerdrum Pedersen</strong></a>, and Associate Professor <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/vsmsc"><strong>Viktor Smith</strong></a>. The CBS team is responsible for Work Package 2 on Innovative and alternative business models and digital tools in rural and remote tourism destinations.</p>
<p><strong>Project organization:</strong><br>Associate professor José María Martín Civantos from the University of Granada, Spain, is the coordinator of SECreTour, which has eleven partner institutions from ten EU and non-EU countries. The project has received funding from the European Union’s Horizon Europe research and innovation program under the grant agreement No. 101132584. It started in March 2024 and will run for 36 months.<br>A kick-off Consortium Meeting was held in Banska Bystrica in June 2024 where all partners visited one of the pilot projects and discussed the approach and next steps. The next project meeting will be held at the French pilot in Bibracte in April 2025.</p>
<p><strong>For more information</strong> on the role of CBS in the project, please contact <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/carsten-jacob-humlebaek" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="673547d6-d36a-48a7-9564-5060cc1d44d0" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Carsten </a>at <a href="mailto:cjh.msc@cbs.dk"><strong>cjh.msc@cbs.dk</strong></a>. Read more about the project at <a href="https://secretourproject.eu/"><strong>secretourproject.eu/ </strong></a>or see the explainer video at <a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4o5K-aBFagk"><strong>www.youtube.com/watch?v=4o5K-aBFagk</strong></a></p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="39662-aurora-2030-advancing-societal-impact-through-research-and-education">
                    Au&shy;rora 2030: Ad&shy;van&shy;cing So&shy;ci&shy;et&shy;al Im&shy;pact through Re&shy;search and Edu&shy;ca&shy;tion
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br><a href="https://aurora-universities.eu/"><strong>Aurora universities</strong></a> is a collaborative initiative among research-intensive European universities aimed at aligning academic excellence with societal relevance. Building upon the foundation of the European Universities Initiative, the <a href="https://aurora-universities.eu/about/aurora-2030/"><strong>Aurora 2030</strong></a> project focuses on four strategic priorities: teaching and learning for societal impact, excellent challenge-based research and innovation, collaboration and engagement through inclusive communities, and sustainability pioneers.&nbsp;</p>
<p>These priorities are addressed through ten work packages and 33 task teams, encompassing areas such as Social Entrepreneurship &amp; Innovation, Digital Society &amp; Global Citizenship, Health &amp; Wellbeing, and Culture: Diversities &amp; Identities. The project aims to develop innovative pedagogies, joint educational programs, and research initiatives that contribute to the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs). &nbsp;</p>
<p><strong>Project team at CBS:</strong><br>As member of Aurora universities CBS plays a key role in the Aurora 2030 project, leading work package on social entrepreneurship and stakeholder engagement. The main goal of this work package is to promote teaching and learning for societal impact, with a special emphasis on developing entrepreneurial skills and mindsets. This involves preparing them to engage with and contribute to society by utilizing innovative pedagogies. Central to this work package are the <a href="https://www.seizmic.eu/"><strong>seizmic</strong></a> activities, e.g., the application of the seizmic SURVEY, an evaluation tool which provides quantitative measures for twelve competencies to create societal impact.&nbsp;</p>
<p>The Aurora team at CBS consists of <a href="http://www.cbs.dk/en/staff/ahumsc"><strong>Anne-Karen Hueske</strong></a> (Institutional Coordinator, Academic co-lead), <a href="http://www.cbs.dk/en/staff/khomsc"><strong>Kai Hockerts</strong></a> (academic lead), Catherine Layolle Busch (International Programme Manager), Wilbert van der Meer (Head of the International Office), Morten Irgens (Vice Dean of Innovation and Impact) and Mareike Buss (RDM Senior Advisor).</p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners:</strong><br>Aurora 2030 brings together a consortium of European universities committed to societal impact through education and research: University of Iceland, University Duisburg-Essen, University Federico II of Naples, Universitat Rovira i Virgili, Universität Innsbruck, Palacký University Olomouc, Vrije Universiteit Amsterdam, Université Paris-Est Créteil , and Copenhagen Business.&nbsp; &nbsp;</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>•&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Grant: Erasmus+<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Project duration: November 1, 2023 – October 31, 2027<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Total funding: €14,399,840<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Project number: 101124312<br>•&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Acronym: Aurora 2030<br>&nbsp;<br><strong>For more information, please contact:</strong><br>Institutional coordinator and academic co-lead for social entrepreneurship and innovation:<a href="http://www.cbs.dk/en/staff/ahumsc"><strong> Anne-Karen Hueske</strong></a>, <a href="mailto:ahu.msc@cbs.dk"><strong>ahu.msc@cbs.dk</strong></a>,<br>Websites: <a href="https://aurora-universities.eu/;"><strong>https://aurora-universities.eu/;</strong></a> <a href="https://www.seizmic.eu/"><strong>https://www.seizmic.eu/</strong></a></p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="1944-planttip---modelling-societal-transitions-and-tipping-points-for-the-green-transition-of-food-consumption-">
                    Plant&shy;Tip - Mod&shy;el&shy;ling so&shy;ci&shy;et&shy;al trans&shy;itions and tip&shy;ping points for the green trans&shy;ition of food con&shy;sump&shy;tion 
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p lang="DA-DK"><strong>The project:&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong><br>The PlantTip project aims to investigate societal tipping points in consumer behaviour in Denmark, with a focus on accelerating the green transition towards a sustainable food system.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK">The aim of PlantTip is to experiment and predict how policy instruments, marketing measures, consumer-oriented innovations, and social dynamics can be combined to trigger societal tipping points. The project employs an innovative approach of developing (a) agent-based models based on (b) social-behavioural experimentation data.&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK">Large-scale and accelerated dietary change could crucially contribute to GHG emission reduction in Denmark. However, the current rate of change towards more sustainable diets is far too slow and restricted to niches. Societal tipping points must be reached to accelerate the needed changes, but we lack knowledge on how to predict and foster large scale behaviour change.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK">Current approaches overemphasise solutions that change food, but not the food-eaters. We argue that a complex systems change is necessary, which this project initiates, by identifying actionable insights about instruments, innovations and social dynamics that can trigger societal tipping points.&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>Collaborative partners:&nbsp;</strong><br>PlantTip is a joint project of Copenhagen Business School, Aarhus University and Democracy X, and collaborates with, amongst others, the Stockholm Resilience Centre and the chair of International Political Economy and Environmental Politics, ETH Zurich.&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>MSC project team:&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong><br>The project team at MSC consists of Associate Professor&nbsp;<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/meike-janssen" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="724c184b-144f-48d9-8eb0-c484f3c3c077" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Meike Janssen</a>&nbsp;and the postdoctoral researchers&nbsp;<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/alice-pizzo" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="21e03f89-17e0-4cf6-a7d3-6c5c8f36108d" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Alice Pizzo&nbsp;</a>and&nbsp;<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/giulia-priolo" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="e00e35e3-14ad-4ced-97be-135d57e4ce6b" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Giulia Priolo</a>.&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK"><strong>The grant:&nbsp;</strong><br>The project is funded by the Novo Nordisk Foundation (9.8 mio DKK), and runs from April 2025 – June 2028&nbsp;</p>
<p lang="DA-DK">For further information, please contact <a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/meike-janssen" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="724c184b-144f-48d9-8eb0-c484f3c3c077" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Meike Janssen</a>,&nbsp;<a href="mailto:mj.msc@cbs.dk">mj.msc@cbs.dk</a>. &nbsp;&nbsp;</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>




  
<article data-component-id="campus:accordionContent" class="component container accordion-content accordion-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
  <details class="accordion-item">
    <summary>
      <h3 class="c-paragraph-2 sb accordion-title" data-urlhash="39664-making-oceans-count-ii-moc2-">
                    Mak&shy;ing Oceans Count II (MOC2) 
      
      </h3>
    </summary>
      <div class="items">
                    


<article  data-component-id="campus:bodyText" class="component row text-component component--view-mode--accordion">
  <div class="grid-two-thirds center text-col body-headings">            <p><strong>The project:</strong><br>"Making Oceans Count II " (MOC2) will develop much-needed blue indicators and metrics for measuring impacts and dependencies from human activities on nature. Ocean assets metrics are parameters or measurements used to assess the health, productivity, and sustainability of ocean resources. Through a systematic data-driven process, MOC2 will address the needs from future regulatory frameworks and ESG standards geared specifically towards the finance sector. With a primary focus on Denmark and the Nordics, the program is poised to generate insights with global relevance for a well-supported, sustainable blue economy.</p>
<p>The project consortium has outlined five strategic work packages to pursue a healthier marine environment in Danish and European waters. The work packages will address the needs of the sustainable blue economy and finance actors, expand the use of asset-level data for ocean activities, unlock scientific marine and environmental data, research key platforms for sharing metrics, and ensure that ESG and finance professionals are equipped to prioritize ocean health.</p>
<p>The long-term objective of the project is to harness the potential of ocean-related data to develop insightful blue metrics for investors and industries affected by ocean conditions. These metrics aim to produce positive impacts on the marine environment and assist these stakeholders in adapting to changes in environmental conditions and regulatory frameworks. The project seeks to bridge the current divide between environmental and climate data, public data platforms, regulatory developments, and the actual practices of the financial and private sectors. This integration is intended to enhance the effective use of data in financing a sustainable blue economy and maintaining healthy marine ecosystems.</p>
<p><strong>Collaborative partners and MSC team:</strong><br>This is a collaborative project between the <a href="https://www.greendigitalfinancealliance.org/"><strong>Green Digital Finance Alliance</strong></a> (GDFA), <a href="http://www.hubocean.earth/"><strong>HUB Ocean</strong></a>, and <a href="http://www.cbs.dk/"><strong>CBS</strong></a>.</p>
<p>Associate Professor <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/kjmsc"><strong>Kristjan Jespersen</strong></a> and Postdoctoral Researcher <a href="https://www.cbs.dk/en/research/departments-and-centres/department-of-management-society-and-communication/staff/kumsc"><strong>Kedar Uttam</strong></a>, both from MSC, represent CBS. Building on their previous involvement in MOC1, the researchers will focus on scoping and addressing the needs of the sustainable blue economy and finance. Their goal is to ensure that environmental, social, and governance (ESG) professionals, as well as those in the financial sector, are well-prepared to integrate ocean health considerations into financial services and blue economy activities.</p>
<p><strong>The grant:</strong><br>The project is funded by the <a href="https://veluxfonden.dk/en"><strong>Velux Foundation</strong></a> and will run from 2024 - 2026</p>
<p><strong>For more information</strong>, contact GDFA at <a href="mailto:ocean@gdfalliance.org"><strong>ocean@gdfalliance.org</strong></a> or<a href="/en/research/departments/department-management-society-and-communication/kristjan-jespersen" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="5b08e43e-4a66-4f48-a140-4824ed7b9bde" data-entity-substitution="canonical"> Kristjan Jespersen</a> at <a href="mailto:kj.msc@cbs.dk"><strong>kj.msc@cbs.dk</strong></a>.</p>

      </div>
</article>


      
      </div>
  </details>
</article>


        </div>
          </div>
  </div>
</article>


            



<article data-component-id="campus:publications" class="component publications container">
      

  <div data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="heading_parent display-heading color-secondary-dark-blue">
                <h2  data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="c-title-1 sb pt-none inherit-text-color" id="publications">
                    Publications
      
      </h2>
              <span class="cta-link desktop">
        

  <a href="https://research.cbs.dk/en/organisations/d8468c05-45ea-477c-8397-49c572707aa2"   data-component-id="campus:button" class="btn btn-hover-scroll">
    <span class="hover-scroll-area" data-replace="See all publications">
      <span class="link-text">
        See all publications
      </span>
      <span class="visually-hidden"></span>

        <h2 class="visually-hidden">Education (Panel content)</h2>

        

  <div  class="component components_spacing tab-content component--view-mode--subsection">
                


  
<article  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

                <h2 id="1953-our-contribution-to-programmes-and-courses" class="two-col-header pt-none c-title-2 sb mb">            Our con&shy;tri&shy;bu&shy;tion to pro&shy;grammes and courses
      </h2>
    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p>MSC contributes to programmes across CBS, with an emphasis on management, sustainability, communication, technology, and international business. Much of our teaching emphasises responsible management, organisation, and governance in a world transformed by globalisation, technology, and environmental degradation.&nbsp;</p>
<p>We bring to our classrooms a particular concern with culture, communication and context to understand and critically reflect on the interaction and interdependencies of business and society. MSC staff are at the forefront of developing new and innovative teaching formats, especially in light of the possibilities and challenges of online teaching, artificial intelligence, and digital media.&nbsp;</p>
</div>
          <div class="body-headings"><p>While MSC contributes to courses across programmes at the bachelor, Master and Executive level, our research environments are particularly strongly represented in some particular programs.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>
<p>At the bachelor level, our faculty play large roles in CBS’ BSc. in Corporate Communication, the BSc. in Market and Cultural Analysis, the BSc. in Business administration and Psychology, the BSc. in Business, Language and Culture, and the BSc. in Digital Management.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>
<p>At the master’s level, we contribute extensively to the MSc. in Strategy, Organization and Leadership and the MSc. Sustainability minor and the MSc. Environmental, Social, Governance minor.&nbsp;</p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</article>


          


  
<article  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

      </h2>
    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p>Scroll down and find it further down this page!</p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</article>

<article  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

                <h2 id="1958-our-phd-programmes" class="two-col-header pt-none c-title-2 sb mb">            Our PhD pro&shy;grammes
      </h2>
    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p><strong>The MSC PhD environment&nbsp;</strong></p>
<p>At MSC, PhD students join a vibrant, international and interdisciplinary research community exploring the complex interplay between business and society. We host 20–30 PhD students, supervised by leading scholars across management, communication, sustainability, technology, development studies, and governance.</p>
<p><br>Through our portfolio of PhD courses, PhD students advance their knowledge of theories and methods tailored to their projects. MSC’s strong interdisciplinary orientation ensures both academic depth and breadth, preparing PhD candidates to address the pressing challenges of business and society.</p>
</div>
          <div class="body-headings"><p>MSC offers a supportive and collegial culture that integrates PhD students into all aspects of academic life. All PhD candidates are affiliated with one of the department’s research centers or clusters and take part in two work-in-progress seminars with expert discussants, annual PhD days, and regular research seminars.&nbsp;</p>
<p>Informal activities such as lunches, peer gatherings and social events foster community spirit and create space for exchange.&nbsp;</p>
<p>MSC predominantly recruits PhD students on a 3-year scheme, funded through CBS, external research grants, industrial PhD collaborations, or partnerships with other academic institutions.</p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</article>


          
<!--2026 Refactored-->








<article data-component-id="campus:mediaText" class="component media-text-component container colorprofile-component component--view-mode--default global-colorprofile-corporate">

  



  <div class="paper-page">
    <div class="content_wrapper row row-no-gap colorprofile flip-grid dark-variant">
      <div class="content display-heading  color-primary-corporate-white">
                  <div data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="color-primary-corporate-white">
                          

  <div data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="heading_parent">
                <h2  data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" class="inherit-text-color a-display-3" id="1959-being-a-phd-student-at-msc">
                    Be&shy;ing a PhD Stu&shy;dent at MSC 
      
      </h2>
        
      </div>

                      </div>
                <div class="content_text">
          <p class="c-manchet-1">            Being a PhD student at MSC means joining a vibrant interdisciplinary community where mentorship extends well beyond your supervisors. Seminars, peer feedback, and informal exchanges create a culture of openness. From early abstract ideas to full manuscripts, there is always someone ready to listen, support, and push you forward. Our doctoral community ensures that you never feel alone in this journey.<br />
<br />
Ahaana Mahanti, MSC PhD student
      </p>
                  </div>
      </div>

              <div class="image cover-img" id="1959-being-a-phd-student-at-msc">
                  <picture>
                  <source srcset="/sites/default/files/styles/content_square_large/public/2025-03/2024_10_09_CBS_Forskere_Undervisere_0046_KK.JPG?h=e5aec6c8&amp;itok=vVd_s_ik 1x" media="all and (min-width: 1278px)" type="image/jpeg" width="1600" height="1600"/>
              <source srcset="/sites/default/files/styles/content_square_large/public/2025-03/2024_10_09_CBS_Forskere_Undervisere_0046_KK.JPG?h=e5aec6c8&amp;itok=vVd_s_ik 1x" media="all and (min-width: 414px) and (max-width: 1277px)" type="image/jpeg" width="1600" height="1600"/>
              <source srcset="/sites/default/files/styles/content_default_square/public/2025-03/2024_10_09_CBS_Forskere_Undervisere_0046_KK.JPG?h=e5aec6c8&amp;itok=II7N-5Td 1x" media="all and (max-width: 413px)" type="image/jpeg" width="750" height="750"/>
                  <img loading="lazy" width="750" height="750" src="/sites/default/files/styles/content_default_square/public/2025-03/2024_10_09_CBS_Forskere_Undervisere_0046_KK.JPG?h=e5aec6c8&amp;itok=II7N-5Td" alt="" />

  </picture>


      

  </div>
          </div>
  </div>

  </article>


          


  
<article  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

                <h2 id="6101-career-paths-for-our-phd-students" class="two-col-header pt-none c-title-2 sb mb">            Ca&shy;reer paths for our Ph.D. stu&shy;dents
      </h2>
    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p>Our department provides a stimulating environment that equips PhD candidates to pursue successful national and international academic careers. Through seminars, expert supervision, and an active and collegial PhD community, our candidates acquire the skills to produce cutting-edge scholarship as well as the soft skills needed to navigate academic labour markets with confidence.</p>
<p>The department’s strong position in the fields of sustainability, business &amp; development studies, communication, governance, and digital transformation further opens doors to international networks and collaborations at an early stage.</p>
</div>
          <div class="body-headings"><p>Beyond academia, our PhD alumni also apply their expertise in a wide range of professional contexts. Our alumni have taken positions in government ministries, development agencies, NGOs, and private-sector firms, where they use their research to inform policy, drive organizational change, and shape sustainable business practices.&nbsp;</p>
<p>As such, our PhDs not only contribute to advancing knowledge but also create impact in society by bridging academic insights with real-world challenges.</p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</article>


          









<article data-component-id="campus:spotPromo" class="component spot-promo-component colorprofile-component component--view-mode--default global-colorprofile-calm">
  <div data-component-id="campus:spotPromo" class="component-wrapper colorprofile dark-variant">
      <div data-component-id="campus:spotPromo" class="row row-no-gap container row-of-two-fixed no-image">
        <div data-component-id="campus:spotPromo" class="content_wrapper display-heading color-primary-corporate-white">
                    <div data-component-id="campus:spotPromo" class="color-primary-corporate-white">
                                        <h2 id="1960-interested-in-a-phd-with-us" class="inherit-text-color">            In&shy;ter&shy;ested in a PhD with us?
      </h2>
                      </div>
          <div class="content_text">

  <div data-component-id="campus:backgroundImageHeader" class="container display-heading inverse-color">
    <h1>
      Centres and Units
    </h1>
  </div>

      <div class="container">
  <div data-drupal-messages-fallback class="hidden"></div>

        <div class="components_spacing">            



<article class="component container paper-pages-seperate content-section-component component--view-mode--default">

    

  <div class="heading_parent" data-component-id="campus:componentHeading">
                              <h2  class="c-title-1 sb pt-none" data-component-id="campus:componentHeading" id="27359-research-environments-at-cbs">
                    Re&shy;search En&shy;vir&shy;on&shy;ments at CBS
      
      </h2>
        
      </div>


    <div class="paper-page">
      
      

<article  class="radio-tablist component-tablist" data-component-id="campus:contentTabs" id="27359-research-environments-at-cbs" data-tablist-id="_7358-tablist">
            

  <div  class="component components_spacing content-section-sub-component component--view-mode--default">
                


  
<div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--subsection">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText">

    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><p>CBS hosts a wide range of research centres, units, and clusters that advance knowledge across disciplines and address key societal and business challenges. These environments bring together researchers who collaborate on long-term agendas, external partnerships, and high-impact academic work.</p>
<p>The list includes department-based centres, research units, and thematic clusters that reflect the breadth of research taking place across CBS.</p>
</div>
          <div class="body-headings"><h3>Cross-Cutting Initiatives</h3>
<p>Most research environments are anchored in one or more departments. A few are cross-cutting initiatives that connect expertise across CBS:<br>&nbsp;</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="/en/research/centres-and-units/cbs-leadership-centre" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="8255f242-da2e-4e0a-868b-ee4d05df30ee" data-entity-substitution="canonical">CBS Leadership</a></li>
<li><a href="/en/research/centres-and-units/cbs-maritime" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="0424f81f-4f98-40ee-af19-5f3702bcc42c" data-entity-substitution="canonical">CBS Maritime</a></li>
<li><a href="/en/research/centres-and-units/green-office" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="47bd3385-793b-4287-a484-ef1049d4d6f6" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Green Office</a></li>
</ul>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
      
  </div>
  </div>
</div>


      
  </div>

      </article>

    </div>

    
</article>

          


  
<div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="component container two-col-text-component component--view-mode--default">
  <div  data-component-id="campus:twoColumnText" class="paper-page">

    
        
<div  class="text_wrapper row row-auto row-gap-xlarge">
                <div class="body-headings"><h3>De­part­ment of Ac­co­un­ting (ACC)</h3>
<ul>
<li><a href="/en/research/departments/department-accounting/center-corporate-governance-ccg" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="855e086a-ccdc-4be0-aaed-c26d4119e75f" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Center for Corporate Governance (CCG)</a></li>
<li><a href="/en/research/departments/department-accounting/center-enterprise-foundations" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="bd1ac5bf-cd4c-4a63-b2f6-cac03162144d" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Cen­ter for En­ter­prise Found­a­tions</a></li>
<li><a href="/en/research/departments/department-accounting/center-owner-managed-businesses" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="def55a12-88df-43bb-996e-9297ea1e225a" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Center for Owner-Managed Businesses</a></li>
</ul>
<h3>De­part­ment of Bu­si­ness Hu­ma­ni­ties and Law (BHL)</h3>
<ul>
<li><a href="/en/about-cbs/profile/strategy/geopolitics" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="770cd7b4-e8f6-4fd1-8b6c-b9b32fc5b7dd" data-entity-substitution="canonical">CBS Geopolitics &amp; Business Security</a></li>
<li><a href="/en/research/departments/department-business-humanities-and-law/cbs-law" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="01756f45-1d41-4d7e-b5dc-67a3fdad6b28" data-entity-substitution="canonical">CBS Law</a></li>
<li><a href="/en/research/departments/department-business-humanities-and-law/centre-business-history" data-entity-type="node" data-entity-uuid="1235b540-0967-46c3-848d-165b7ad39fc4" data-entity-substitution="canonical">Centre for Business History</a></li>
